# Findings → Implications

**What this doc is.** The generative middle layer between the experiment
ledgers and the theory registry. Ledgers record verdicts; theories.md
records what crystallized; this doc holds the step between — what a
billed result *means*, and where it leads. Breakthroughs happen here:
implications from separate findings conjoin (F3 × T153 produced the
demand-pull thesis and PC-8-D in one afternoon), and next steps / organ
candidates fall out of the conjunctions, not the findings alone.

**Discipline.** An entry is written when a verdict bills (C13 three-way:
FALSIFIED / VOID / UNTESTED — voids enter too, as method-findings; an
instrument lesson is a finding about the program). Every finding cites
its receipt. Implications are marked speculative until tested. When an
implication crystallizes into a theory, constraint, organ, or spec, the
entry is stamped DISCHARGED with the pointer and kept as history. Cross-
reference findings by ID — conjunctions are where the yield is.

Types: **[W]** world-finding (about the organism/environment),
**[M]** method-finding (about the program's instruments).

---

## Open findings

### F1 [W] The interface wants states, not schedules
**Finding:** Forecast *timing* of the next surprise buys no Router
efficiency; ambient surprise *level* (permutation-invariant content)
buys a lot — live beat zeroed baseline in both paired seeds but lost to
time-permuted placebo in both, and placebo crushed baseline
(results/p45_indomain.json, first valid P45(a) verdict).
**Rhymes with:** Phase-1's rank-rarity survival (absolute constructions
failed; self-relative rank worked); biology's interoception (cortisol is
a level, not an appointment).
**Implications:**
- The obs-fold vocabulary should be stative/self-relative summaries —
  barometers, not almanacs. The predictor's value migrates offline
  (grammar learning, diagnostics); the interface wants low-order
  statistics.
- Open decomposition: the placebo effect's pathway — (a) input-
  perturbation regularization during policy training vs (b) marginal-
  distribution information (knowing typical surprise magnitudes). The
  static-noise arm (matched-marginal, no structure) discriminates.
**Leads to:** redesign of obs[400:408] toward ambient-level channels;
static-noise decomposition arm (queued, diagnostic priority).

### F2 [W] Levels are insulated — a lever only moves its own level
**Finding:** Three independent instances: store-level certainty release
never touched the coactivation-level anxiety loop (stable across two
full CV-P1 runs); motor-level delegation never touched thought-type
composition (PC-8-X); engine-level staleness never touched fitness while
an oracle-taught policy held behavior (CV-P1(c) VOID diagnosis).
**Implications:**
- Empirical support for T144's separation (organs couple only through
  narrow media) — but it CUTS AGAINST the conversion organ spec's
  inherited assumption that the anxiety loop is store-curable ("cognitive
  cancer" needs apoptosis in the RIGHT tissue).
- Retro-explains T57: release worked there because certainty→obs→behavior
  was a closed same-level loop in that harness.
- The conversion organ's effectors may need to be level-indexed — one
  governance loop per stratum — rather than one organ reaching across
  stores. (Speculative; touches the organ-vs-federation-metabolism open
  question in conversion_organ_requirements.md.)
**Leads to:** CV-P1(d) (RUNNING — policy frozen at shift, engine is the
only adapting component: lever and pathology same-level).

### F3 [W] Ascent is demand-pulled, not slack-pushed
**Finding:** Internal delegation is profitable without producing
cognitive ascent — coverage as exogenous treatment: coordination share
of residual deliberation flat, absolute coordination rate falls with
coverage, uncapped arm best fitness (results/pc8_exogenous.json).
**Conjunction with T153:** freed capacity is not redeployed because the
plain world contains nothing coordination-priced to spend it on. The
environment organism's complexity pressure is the ladder's missing rung
— T153 is the enabling condition for the floor-raising chain to
language, not a side project. Also separates delegation types: an
external holder embeds its own demand (monitoring, trust, negotiation —
the PC-9 margin IS a coordination problem); a shortcut frees steps, a
holder frees a responsibility.
**Leads to:** PC-8-D (BUILT+SMOKED, queued — the 2x2's rich cells:
delegation x demand-rich world; delegation axis newly integrated into
the rich runner); external-delegation arm (designed direction, after
PC-8-D); EL-0 priority raised sharply.
**PC-8-D result (2026-08-09, seed 42): INCONCLUSIVE on ascent, decisive
on profit.** The demand x delegation interaction on FITNESS is enormous:
+10,476 in the rich world (survival flips from −9,749 to +727) vs
~+2,300 in the plain world — demand makes delegation MATTER. Ascent:
share delta +0.039 (4x the plain +0.009, predicted direction) and
better per-coverage absolute retention (−0.40/unit vs −0.58/unit), but
the pre-registered bar (absolute delta > 0) not met; single seed. NEW
CONFOUND caught (C20-2 flavor, named before billing): arms differ
wildly in somatic state, and somatic groups sit in the composition
denominator — "less pain" can masquerade as "more coordination."
Next iteration: somatic-state-conditioned composition + a second seed.
The demand-pull thesis stands strengthened on the profit axis and
unresolved on the ascent axis.

### F4 [W] Sophistication pays offline; simplicity pays online
**Finding:** Linkage (a counting statistic) matched or beat perturbation
sensitivity for discovery ordering (phase3_race, only valid seed);
ambient level beat the transformer's forecast at the interface (F1); the
stuckness fix was a count-weighted reward mean. Meanwhile the expensive
machinery kept winning offline: grammar gates passed, diagnostic exports
(receptor gaps, conflation candidates) have no cheap substitute.
**Implication:** metabolic-rent doctrine as data — route cheap statistics
to the behavioral boundary, spend computation only where no summary
suffices (diagnosis, offline learning). Design heuristic for every
future fold-back: ask "what is the cheapest statistic that carries
this?" before folding the model output itself.
**Leads to:** linkage priority adopted as the GBM ordering signal;
standing design heuristic for organ interfaces.

### F5 [W] "Stuck" means past its EARNING — support is not pathology
**Finding:** v1 stuckness (support x usage x no-movement) targeted
load-bearing knowledge — certainty is an outcome-match EMA, so "support"
selects clusters currently predicting well; releasing them hurt fitness
with 40% LESS volume than global spray. v2's yield factor (count-
weighted reward) removed the harm in both paired seeds
(conversion_organ_requirements.md ledger).
**Implications:**
- The Discovery-Pathway conflation (ripe-for-annealing vs load-bearing-
  and-stable) is empirically forced, exactly as the spec pre-named.
- Confidence-shaped signals cannot detect pathology among the confident;
  only the organism's own currency (reward/yield) separates earned
  stability from confident failure. Likely generalizes to any organ
  whose receptors read certainty-shaped quantities.
**Leads to:** v2 detector standing; CV-P1 proper still UNTESTED —
CV-P1(d) is the first level-matched attempt.

### F6 [W] Stuckness accumulates without governance
**Finding:** The no-anneal control accumulates stuck-scored clusters
across generations (0.05 → 3.7, s4242) with declining absolute movement
(0.041 → 0.023) — CV-P1 run 1 side observation (rank-based counts;
directional only).
**Implication:** CV-P2's ablation-cost prediction has its first
directional evidence, free. A dedicated CV-P2 run could bill it.
**Leads to:** CV-P2 candidate when the conversion organ queue reopens.

### F7 [W] The permanence world's surprise grammar is hyperlearnable
**Finding:** In-domain kind-head accuracy 0.981 vs marginal 0.521 at
only 554 tokens (phase3_race s42) — by far the largest kind-grammar
margin any corpus has produced (rich world: 0.852 vs 0.820).
**Implication (speculative):** environments organized around a single
sharp hidden distinction produce sharply learnable surprise grammars —
grammar learnability may INDEX the conceptual structure of the world,
which is exactly the quantity the environment organism's complexity
pressure would need to sense. A candidate measurement bridge between
T153's language refinement and the surprise economy.
**Leads to:** nothing scheduled; flagged for the EL design (grammar
learnability as a produced-learnable-surprise component).

### F10 [W] The organism's knowledge is indexical — layout shifts cannot create staleness
**Finding:** Mirroring the endorphin sources produced NO visible
control-arm fitness drop even with the policy FROZEN (CV-P1(d) VOID,
data/cvp1_shiftF_*; g5 within the ±40% per-gen noise band both seeds) —
the oracle-absorption diagnosis from CV-P1(c) cannot explain the frozen
case.
**Implication:** the store maps SENSED FIELD CONFIGURATIONS to outcome
deltas; it never encoded locations. The sensed-context→outcome physics
is unchanged by relocating sources, so a gradient-following organism's
knowledge cannot go stale from layout change — its epistemology is
here-and-now-indexed (rhymes with F1: states not schedules; and with
T126 read precisely: the environmental structure made visible is
field-signature structure, not coordinates). Staleness requires a
CONTINGENCY inversion: the same sensed context producing the opposite
outcome. Corollary: "environment shift" experiments across the program
must shift contingencies, not layouts.
**Leads to:** CV-P1(e) — swap pain and endorphin source ROLES at the
shift (identical signatures, opposite valence; every stored mapping
about them wrong in sign; reward-seeking walks into punishment, so the
control-arm drop follows almost by construction).

### F8 [M] Measurement and intervention share substrate by default
**Finding:** Three contaminations in one session: annealing writes the
certainty the movement metric reads; shortcuts zero the channels the
composition metric reads; filtering the pool moves a share by
arithmetic. Plus the global-RNG pairing leak and the oracle-teacher
absorbing planted shifts.
**Implication:** in a closed-loop architecture, endpoint independence
must be engineered per-experiment, the way C1 engineered it for
imagination/belief. DISCHARGED → C20 (pre-flight) and C13 (earned
verdicts, three-way vocabulary). Kept here because the pattern predicts
where the NEXT contamination will appear: any new organ's first
instrument should be assumed contaminated until the C20-2 question is
answered in writing.

### F9 [M] Salience windows make discovery races hard to instrument
**Finding:** The attribution race was saturated at 10 sites (gap = the
biggest store structure), degenerate at the size-rank-1 boundary, and
gap-clusters vanished entirely for some seeds at 5 sites — the window
between too-obvious and not-found was razor-thin
(results/phase3_race*.json).
**Implication:** a fair seeded gap must be experienced often-but-
DIFFUSELY — frequent enough to enter the store, spread enough that bulk
statistics don't concentrate it. Spatially concentrated pain structures
can't do this (dwelling manufactures salience). Design note for any
future discovery-cost experiment, and a hint about what makes REAL
receptor gaps hard: the ones that matter are precisely the non-salient
ones.

### F11 [W] The designed genome prior is broadly earned — and revision needs a richer verdict vocabulary than earned/dead
**Finding:** EX-0 retrospective audit (results/genome_audit_ex0.md):
185 receptors ever-discovered across 257 archives; 24/25 families have
earned receipts; ZERO dead families. But: procedural_memory has no
by-name receipts despite its mechanism being demonstrably load-bearing
(shortcuts pervade the archives) — its receptors were never wired into
any battery; and visual_pattern looks dead under a naive join while
having activated abundantly under its own schema.
**Implications:**
- T154's genome-revision rung needs five verdicts, not two: earned /
  dead / **uninstrumented** (receipts exist but are not addressable) /
  **schema-fragmented** (receipts exist under a different vocabulary) /
  thin. The unpredicted categories are bookkeeping states, which
  suggests the revision rung's first moves in ANY system will be
  bookkeeping moves — the ontology is usually better than its records.
- The strict "fired at all" audit is impossible retrospectively because
  the activation mask was never persisted by name — an F8-flavored
  lesson at the archive layer: what isn't serialized by name cannot be
  audited by name. Instrumentation-for-auditability is a design
  requirement, not an afterthought.
- The five thin families (social, regulatory, epistemic, observation,
  logic) are EX-2's sharpest targets: expression search will say
  whether their unearned receptors are latent-waiting or unearnable in
  the current worlds.
**Leads to:** forward instrumentation (mask-by-name; per-family battery
persistence); wiring procedural_memory's four receptors into the
battery; registry hygiene pass (one authoritative family list); EX-2
aimed at the thin families.
**User's extensions (2026-08-09, registered into T154):** (1) the
BOOKKEEPING-LAG PRINCIPLE — capability runs ahead of accounting in any
multi-rung system; first upward-flow signal is always "records don't
match reality"; C4's overwrite-corruption has a slow sibling,
OMISSION-corruption, and the junction law gains its third clause:
receipts must be lived, re-validated, AND ADDRESSABLE. (2) UNEARNABLE
IS A CLAIM ABOUT THE ENVIRONMENT — the thin families are tier-9
specification: the organism's unearned vocabulary is the environment
organism's curriculum backlog (T154–T153 coupled at the top; upward
flow in the organism's ladder terminates as demand on the world's
complexity pressure). Corollary: DELETION REQUIRES THE WORLD'S
TESTIMONY — a family is deletable only after an environment containing
its named causal structure was offered and it still failed to earn.
Falsifiable: build the tier a thin family names; its receptors activate
(environment-spec reading confirmed) or don't (deletion finally
licensed, with testimony).

### F12 [W] Release works against real staleness — and the response must match the pathology's shape
**Finding:** CV-P1(e), the first billed CV-P1 verdict (floors −51%/−64%,
both seeds): certainty release beats no-release under planted global
staleness 2/2 (gen-6 recovery: annealing arms near pre-shift, control
~−26%) — T57 confirmed in controlled form. But global beat targeted
2/2: the full hedonic inversion makes staleness GLOBAL, so spray
matched the pathology's distribution and the scalpel had no privileged
target (data/cvp1_invert_*).
**Implications:**
- The conversion organ's targeting capacity was mis-specified as
  "targeted always beats global." The organ-grade capacity is
  RELEASE MATCHED TO THE PATHOLOGY'S DISTRIBUTION — which requires
  sensing that distribution, i.e., the stuckness receptor's real job
  is estimating the staleness FIELD's shape, not just ranking clusters.
  (Speculative: this makes the organ's first de-conflation
  global-vs-local staleness — a level question, served by ABSOLUTES,
  the CV-0 scope lesson recurring at the organ's core.)
- F10 validated empirically: contingency inversion produced in one
  generation what two layout shifts could not produce at all.
- Matrix status: global staleness → spray wins (e); no staleness →
  targeting harmless (b); LOCAL staleness → untested, the decisive
  cell (CV-P1(f), partial inversion).
**Leads to:** CV-P1(f) → VOID-CEILING-COLLAPSE (a new failure subtype
for the C20 catalog: converting endorphin to pain with nothing added
back left a hedonically bankrupt world — fitness at −180% to −360%
with no recovery possible in ANY arm; the intervention rewrote not the
measured quantity but its ATTAINABLE RANGE. C20-2's cousin: check that
the endpoint's reference frame survives the intervention). CV-P1(g)
FINAL (four seeds): localized cell at CHANCE across all comparisons
(t>g 2/4, t>c 2/4, g>c 1/4) — an honest null with floors passed. THE
COMPLETED MATRIX: none → release harmless at best; global → release
decisive 2/2 (T57 confirmed AND scoped); localized → the store
SELF-HEALS (per-entry certainty EMA corrects stale entries in passing
while valid knowledge keeps behavior functional). Synthesis: release's
value is UNLOCKING EXPLORATION WHEN THE WHOLE MODEL IS WRONG — held
certainty imprisons behavior under global failure; under local failure
ordinary annealing suffices. The organ's release effector reduces to a
dose policy triggered by GLOBAL model-failure detection — sensors
already exist (meta-error channel 404; detector absolutes). The
scalpel's benefit, if any, is below every current harness's noise
floor. Fifth instance of no-fixed-balance (release mode across
staleness distributions). CV-P1 CLOSED at this scale, fully billed.

### F13 [W] At trunk grain, the world refuses closure — and every refusal is differentiation demand

**Finding (2026-08-10, seed-44 baseline, the first citable SOV run):**
50 generations, full rich stack, fixed 16-operator substrate. 1.54M
receipts, conservation PASS in-loop all 49 audited generations (Law 1
positive-mass, Law 2 global etymology, Law 3 grounding samples,
imagination boundary — P63's first at-scale receipt). **195 closure
attempts, 195 retractions, zero survivors: all 33 inherited slots open
after 200k lived steps.** Closures form on transient local tightness and
die on the next diverse evidence (~4 reopens/gen, steady). Behavior
normal (164 unique receptors, fitness peak 47.8k at gen 49, thinking
influence to pcorr 0.49) — the observer web cost nothing. The voided
run's "3 closed" is now doubly refuted: those were coordinate artifacts
of encoder drift.

**Implications:**
1. **Family-grain slots SHOULD fail to close — they are conflations.**
   "Causality," "compression," "perception" are not one thing each; a
   slot that is secretly several distinctions cannot converge to one
   centroid under honest evidence. The 195 retractions are the web
   discovering this repeatedly. **The reopen stream is therefore the
   funding signal for the replay-phase Differentiate scans** — the
   baseline didn't just validate the substrate, it generated the demand
   ledger for the next build: slots ranked by retraction count are slots
   ranked by de-conflation strain.
2. The Einstein principle, enforced by the world rather than by policy:
   at this grain and exposure, nothing is inevitable. Resolution needs
   finer slots (Differentiate/Individuate) or richer worlds (T153's
   curriculum) — closure-attempt survival ratio becomes an instrument:
   a gauge of slot-grain-vs-world-complexity match.
3. The closure criterion (radius EMA ≤ 0.05, ≥3 lived fits) is eager,
   but eagerness is now safe: the 404 lifecycle self-corrects at ~5
   generations' latency. Closure quality is measurable as
   attempts-vs-survivors, not assumed.
4. Instrumentation gap (fix before E1): unassigned-pool size was not
   persisted in gen records — the web-level 404 stream's volume (the
   Individuate demand signal) is unrecoverable for this run. (Fixed
   same day in both runners.)

**Follow-on implications (2026-08-10, from holding F13 against the
corpus):**
5. **The trunk is a question set, not a concept set — observed.** 33
   slots ran 200k steps as a standing option book: perpetually funded,
   continuously re-validated, never rationally exercised. Permanent
   productive ignorance held without collapse — SOV's central claim in
   the wild.
6. **Two-axis grain-match instrument** (with T154's thin-family
   signal): starved slots (never fire) = world too poor → T153's
   curriculum backlog; churning slots (close-and-retract) = world too
   rich for the grain → Differentiate demand; closed-surviving =
   resolved (currently EMPTY class — itself the measurement). The
   mutual-Umwelt coupling now has its gauge.
7. **Conflation vs non-stationarity — the rival reading and its
   discriminator, pre-registered:** split the highest-churn slot;
   conflation predicts children churn less (split named a real
   distinction); non-stationarity predicts children churn identically
   with temporally-clustered fail windows (a world-regime clock for
   T153). This is the replay-phase build's acceptance test.
8. **Language must begin with questions — compulsion, not permission.**
   At trunk grain there are no knowns to share: an LC readout over
   this web emits zero assertions. E1 expectation: near-pure
   co-questioning (poses dominant, closure broadcasts ≈ 0). P72's
   calibration claim previewed before LC exists.
9. **Local-pathology-heals-locally now holds at two layers** (store:
   CV-P1 final matrix; web: 195 local retractions, zero global
   resets). Second organ, same law — suggests architectural, not
   incidental.
10. Exports: persist PER-SLOT retraction counts (the demand ledger's
    ranking is the actionable part); write split-reduces-churn into the
    replay-phase requirements as acceptance.

### F14 [N] Schedule-dependence was found inside the "confluent" fragment — schedule-independence is engineered, not conferred

**Finding (2026-08-10, external review + fix):** Anneal — a member of
the monotone fragment the confluence "theorem" declared order-
independent — compounded its decay under repeated invocation:
annealing every 20 steps through step 200 gave 0.110 where annealing
once gave 0.664. A live counterexample to the claimed property, inside
the fragment claimed safe. Fixed (decay from the last application, not
the last fit), and the fix is the first *relation* imposed on the
etymology monoid's presentation (`anneal(t₁);anneal(t₂) = anneal(t₂)`).

**Implications:** the confluence claim was downgraded to a conjecture
under P66; schedule-independence is a per-operator property to be
engineered and tested, never assumed from the word "monotone"; and the
monoid-presentation program (which relations hold?) and P66 are the
same project approached from two ends — the categorical resolution
(graded writer monad) made this identification exact.

### Registration cascade (2026-08-10) — index entry

One day, three external reviews, five theory arrivals — recorded here
because the doc's charter is tracking where breakthroughs come from:
- **Individuate + Retract** registered (review 1 found the two empty
  cells of P67's coverage table; Individuate = the program's own
  receptor-discovery move, missing from its own algebra).
- **Suspend + Counterposit** registered (the user's "rationally closed,
  hypothetically open"; review 2 added the modal axis, ATMS
  justification environments — the ledger became a truth-maintenance
  engine — and abduction-by-replay: the C4 log IS Pearl's background
  context, making rung 3 a bounded replay).
- **The counterfactual license + voucher** (the user's principle:
  completeness is the rung-3 precondition; answers now ship their
  warrants — implemented).
- **T155, the Language Center** (generation as ledger readout, not log
  prediction; phrase-scale, structure-indexed; calibration by
  construction; P72–P74).
- **Bind + P75** (the user's resonance thread, de-mystified as common
  cause: the 16th operator, first communal object, COORDINATED
  provenance; CoFitTracker live in E1).
Census: 16 named operators, 12 geometry + 9 fiber primitives, P48–P75
under single authority. The generative pattern, three times over: an
external critic goes looking for missing structure and finds the
framework already contains its own repair — the gaps close from inside.

### F15 [N] The generation-grain processing stream is memoryless — the tower's first carving was too coarse

**Finding (2026-08-10, P78 first bill, pre-registered):** walk-forward
over the seed-44 baseline's processing stream (n=36 predictions):
persistence MAE 4.81, expanding mean 3.83, AR(1) 3.98, carved-state
meta-model 4.17. **Nothing beats the unconditional mean** — the
aggregate reopen count per generation carries no gen-to-gen memory
(same for the secondary target). Verdict: P78 NOT SUPPORTED at
generation grain, per pre-registered rules.

**Implications:**
1. The two readings, discriminated by the open per-slot form: either
   the scalar-per-gen count is a maximally lossy quotient that destroys
   slot-level structure, or there is no meta-structure at all (T156's
   full falsifier). *Correction (2026-08-10): concentration of reopens
   in specific slots is PREDICTED by F13's conflation reading, not yet
   measured — the baseline predates the churn instrumentation. The
   per-slot arm therefore tests both whether concentration exists and
   whether it is predictable.*
2. Consistent with the tower's own economics rather than against them:
   TS-4 says level-2 receptors feed on statistics; the minimum possible
   carving earned nothing — carvings must be fine enough to carry
   receipts.
3. Discipline note: sixth registration of the day, first negative bill
   of the day — the funding law applied to its own newest theory within
   the hour of registration.

**Follow-on implications (2026-08-10, from holding F15 against the
corpus):**
4. **The verdict is a positive receipt for T153's necessity.** A
   memoryless constant-rate reopen stream is what two stationary
   systems facing each other produce: frozen tier-4 machinery meeting a
   frozen 33-slot grain. Flat, memoryless restructuring IS stagnation
   seen from one level up — the static-world flat-line diagnosis now
   has a needle. P79 candidate (pre-register when EL exists): under a
   living environment the reopen process gains temporal structure
   (trend with the complexity ratchet, regimes with curriculum moves).
5. **The tower's law refined: meta-prediction pays in proportion to
   base non-stationarity.** The expanding mean winning IS the funded
   SOV' of a stationary stream — one number, priced correctly in 36
   predictions. TS-4's log-bound is the tower's ceiling (data volume);
   base non-stationarity is its floor (what's worth building).
   Introspection over a stationary mind in a stationary world is rent
   without yield — reflection organs are funded by change, and
   **stagnation propagates up the tower**: a dead world starves every
   level above it. The living environment funds the whole stack.
6. **The inverted closure gradient.** F13: nothing closes at base grain
   (the step-grain world is too rich). F15: the mean closes instantly
   at meta grain ("reopen rate ≈ 4/gen" — arguably the program's first
   legitimately closable K, one level up). Together: **closure happens
   at the level where stationarity lives; abstraction levels exist to
   locate the invariants of the level below.** Each rung is a
   stationarity detector for the rung beneath. Prediction: the
   program's first surviving closures will be meta-closures before
   base-closures — F13's empty class fills from above.
7. Export: the reopen rate's memorylessness becomes a standing
   **regime-change alarm** at zero cost — future deviation from
   flat-memoryless means the world moved (EL online, environment
   shift) or the topology moved (splits landing). A null converted
   into an instrument.

### F16 [W] E1 verdicts: the world writes the same receipt twice; the channel's value hides where the endpoints couldn't see; and co-questioning is myopic

**Findings (2026-08-11, E1 complete: 5 billed arms + gate, seeds 45/46,
all arm-invariance checks PASS, 0 conservation violations across all
runs):**

1. **P75 SUPPORTED, both seeds, decisively.** Channel-free (ISOLATED)
   co-fit: matched 0.133 / 0.128 vs shuffled 0.007 / 0.008 — an ~18x
   separation at n≈14,000 series per seed (floors 200). And the SHARE
   arm's co-fit is numerically identical (0.1327) — **the channel
   contributed nothing to co-fit**, confirming pure world-mediation by
   direct comparison. Resonance's precursor is real: same-geometry
   slots across organisms co-fire because the world already wrote the
   same receipt twice. Bind's admission story has empirical ground.
2. **P64 NOT SUPPORTED as registered** (interior maximum). Seed 45:
   VOID-instrument (divergence ladder non-monotone — manipulation check
   failed, own rules applied). Seed 46 (valid): yield rises
   MONOTONICALLY to the far endpoint (0.681 → 0.723) — only the
   inverted-U's RISING limb was observed. Scoping conjunction (C-iii):
   the shared encoder/trunk/world — the coordination equilibrium E1
   granted for free — compressed the divergence range below
   incommensurability; the falling limb lives at COORDINATE divergence
   (different encoders, bodies, species — or T153's organism↔
   environment case), not family-mask divergence. Within a shared code,
   more divergent partners are simply more valuable, monotonically.
3. **E1-DEMAND NOT SUPPORTED at the pre-registered endpoints** — SHARE
   and ISOLATED identical on closures (1/1, 0/0), sighted radius
   (equal to 4dp), certainty (exactly equal, as the firewall
   guarantees). Yet corroboration ran 64–72%: the testimony was largely
   TRUE. Three independent endpoint starvations explain the null:
   closures don't survive at family grain (F13's regime); the radius
   EMA is frozen by thousands of lived fits (late centroid aims cannot
   move it); certainty was firewalled BY DESIGN. **The endpoint-
   independence firewall also removed every channel through which real
   benefit could register.** Methodological export — C20 gains a sixth
   consideration: *endpoint sensitivity* — verify at least one endpoint
   CAN move through a legal causal path at the magnitudes the
   intervention produces. E2 endpoint: per-generation fresh-fit
   tightness (this gen's fit distances), not lifetime EMA.
4. **Blind coverage: ZERO across 3,600 poses (both seeds).** The
   starvation term never won a top-3 slot — near-misses and 404s on
   sighted slots always outrank holes. **Co-questioning is myopic under
   small budgets: organisms ask about what they struggle with, never
   what they're blind to.** The jigsaw pose ("a hole I cannot fill")
   requires reserved budget — E2: a curiosity quota (one of Q reserved
   for the starved class).
5. Cumulative P63 support: five more full runs, zero conservation
   violations, arm-invariance exact everywhere.

**E2 design exports:** sensitive endpoints (fresh-fit tightness);
curiosity quota; coordinate-divergence ladder (different encoders — or
wait for the T153 inter-species case, where incommensurability is
native); demand retest AFTER the replay phase gives closure a chance at
post-split grain.

**Follow-on implications (2026-08-11, from holding F16 against the
corpus):**
6. **Recognition precedes communication; language monetizes pre-existing
   co-structure rather than creating it.** The bootstrapping circle is
   partially pre-solved by common cause (trunk + territory) — the floor
   of mutual intelligibility is inherited and world-given; Pose/Attest
   trade on that capital. T155's cross-organism intelligibility now
   rests on a measured quantity.
7. **The demand null is the bookkeeping-lag law's third instance**
   (with EX-0 and the C4 lesson): true testimony arrived; no instrument
   could bill it. Value arrives before the accounting that registers
   it. Demand theory = untested-at-depth; proper test located
   post-replay-phase (closure-capable grain).
8. **The pose floor — the floor family's third member.** Probe floor
   (habits), expression floor (blindnesses in expression), now the
   curiosity quota (blindnesses in inquiry): one law three times —
   *receipt-driven economies starve whatever produces no receipts;
   floors are the constitutional correction.* Evidence-driven attention
   neglects structured absences: the noisy-TV dissolution's dark twin
   (D1 disengages from noise — and also from silence, where the
   highest-value unknowns hide).
9. **E1 measured intra-paradigm sociology**: within one code, the most
   divergent colleague pays most, monotonically, and no one asks about
   their own blind spots. The falling limb = Kuhnian incommensurability,
   located BETWEEN codes — natively present only in the
   organism↔environment pair.
10. **P75's magnitude is a calibrator**: measured co-fit is an
    empirical estimate of world-mediated alignment → Attest's Stage-1
    prior and Bind's admission prior can be EARNED from co-fit data
    instead of stipulated. One more constant becomes a measurement.
11. **The convergence: four findings from three experiments all demand
    EL-0** — F13 (starved slots = curriculum backlog), F15 (static
    world flat-lines the tower), F16-P64 (falling limb needs native
    incommensurability), F16-DEMAND (needs curriculum + post-split
    grain). The second species is now overdetermined; EL-0 moves from
    next-in-queue to the program's declared center of gravity.

### F17 [W] The seed language saturates tier 4 — and the junction law is a replication requirement

**Findings (2026-08-11, EL-2 v3, PASS-CONSERVATIVE 4/4):**
1. Across 5 language generations, 5 refinement proposals each passed a
   0.02-nat world-held-out margin within their generation — and **all 5
   were rejected at ratification on the next generation's fresh
   worlds.** The only surviving move was the planted decoy's merge
   (correct). Final held-out NLL: evolved ≡ gen-0 exactly.
2. Verdict meaning: the court is honest and **tier-4's descriptive
   slack is already saturated by the seed vocabulary** — no finer word
   is TRUE at this grain. The language is world-limited, not
   mechanism-limited.

**Implications:**
1. **The fourth convergence on world enrichment.** F13 (family slots
   can't close — world too rich for the grain OR grain awaiting
   curriculum), F15 (static world → stationary meta-statistics), F16
   (falling limb unreachable within shared codes), now F17 (no finer
   word is fundable at tier 4). Four findings, four instruments, one
   demand: **the complexity ratchet must turn.** The living-language
   loop (lexical growth CHANGING generated worlds — EL-0's interpreter
   is the generation side, the coupling is the build) is now the
   program's most-demanded artifact from both sides of the membrane.
2. **The junction law is a replication norm.** T154's re-validation
   clause, applied to the lexicon, is exactly science's replication
   requirement — and it measurably worked: 5/5 selection-inflated
   gains (winner's curse at n≈80-window margins) were caught by a
   single independent world-batch. Registerable methodological export:
   any receipts-funded admission over MANY candidates needs a
   ratification batch — selection and confirmation must never share
   evidence (the two-batch border is multiple-comparisons control,
   stated constitutionally).
3. **T153's asymmetry, observed:** the environment cannot die and its
   moves are never bets — and here it correctly made NO move rather
   than a false one. An immortal teacher can afford patience; the
   editorial (not mortal) enforcement produced exactly the
   conservative stall the theory predicts for a world whose students
   haven't outgrown its vocabulary.

### F18 [W] The ratchet turns; ratification at threshold is stochastic; and one word keeps asking to be split

**Findings (2026-08-11, EL-2.5 v1+v2):**
1. **LL-A PASS, replicated:** utterance-driven world generation under
   describable-slack pressure enriches worlds — evolved populations
   exceed seed populations by +0.026 (v1, 4 gens) and +0.081 nats (v2,
   8 gens) on fresh episodes, floors 0.02, no degeneracy, every
   utterance receipted. **The living loop's generation side works:
   worlds are corpora, mutation is utterance, and selection by
   conditional-structure slack produces measurably richer worlds.**
2. **LL-B VOID-instrument at single-session scale:** v2's seed control
   RATIFIED the hidden-state split that F17 rejected 5/5 at larger
   sample, while the enriched arm proposed-but-failed. A one-shot
   binary contrast (evolved >= 1, seed = 0) cannot bear the claim:
   **ratification at tier-4 grain is threshold-stochastic.** F17
   refined accordingly: tier-4's refinable structure is not absent —
   it sits AT the court's threshold, marginally fundable, crossing on
   some draws. LL-B's honest form is a ratification-RATE comparison
   over repeated independent court sessions (pre-registered for the
   overnight living-language run).
3. **The program's most robust de-conflation demand:** 'under hidden
   state 0' was proposed in EVERY court session of every run — F17's
   five generations, both v2 arms — always from fresh receipts. When
   the first word splits, it will be this one; EL-1's localization has
   now been confirmed by seven independent proposal events.
4. **Enrichment is not concentration:** v2's evolved worlds gained
   overall slack while the specific candidate's ratification signal
   weakened — adding structure can DILUTE the particular conditional
   effect a pending word needs. Implication for the generation
   objective: directed complexity — weight slack in the contexts the
   language's pending words name (the curriculum-backlog logic, T154's
   coupling, applied to the environment's own vocabulary). The
   environment shouldn't just grow richer; it should grow richer WHERE
   its language is trying to learn to speak.

**Exports:** rate-based LL-B design (N court sessions per arm,
independent world batches, compare ratification rates with floors);
directed-slack generation objective as the pre-registered v3 mechanism;
both belong to the overnight EL-P1 run.

**Follow-on implications (2026-08-11, F17+F18 held together):**
5. **Cross-membrane demand alignment — the new decisive experiment.**
   F13's churn ranking (organism-side de-conflation demand) and F18's
   proposal stream (language-side de-conflation demand) are both
   waiting on the same world. Test: does the organism's top-churn slot
   name the same structure as the language's most-proposed word
   ('under hidden state 0' / the hidden-variable machinery)? Aligned →
   one shared demand ledger read from opposite sides; EL-P1
   near-guaranteed; Bind's precursor at the demand level (two
   organisms holding the same question). Misaligned → co-complexification
   is a real risk hypothesis, learned cheaply. Instruments exist
   (per-slot churn + court logs); needs one instrumented run.
6. **Demand-weighted allocation — the fifth instance of one law.**
   Pose floor (F16: attention starves silent classes) + enrichment ≠
   concentration (F18: generation starves pending words) = evidence-
   driven economies neglect what they are trying to learn next, because
   pending things emit weak signals by definition. Every future funding
   economy (receptor discovery, phrase store, imagination budget,
   stakeholder sampling) will need its quota/direction term. Teaching-
   along-the-fringe was this law applied to students; directed slack is
   the teacher applying it to its own vocabulary.
7. **Threshold stochasticity derives the sociolinguistics:** lexical
   change is a rate phenomenon (noisy evidence + admission thresholds),
   integrable only over many sessions — why real language change is
   slow and population-mediated, and why timescale separation is the
   integration window the noise REQUIRES, not just a safety constraint.
8. **Two towers feeding each other:** describable slack is a level-2
   statistic of inhabitants — the environment's funding signal IS its
   SOV' over the organisms (T156's third-host confirmation). EL-3
   inscription gives organisms the reciprocal tower (reading = access
   to the teacher's meta-level). The stack meets in the middle.
9. **T153's constitution survived construction unamended** across five
   built-and-accepted phases: honest stall (F17), funded growth (F18),
   mirror trap unfundable by objective shape. A constitution needing no
   amendments during its own implementation is the strongest receipt a
   proposal can earn.

### F19 [W] Aimed enrichment funds vocabulary; unaimed enrichment funds nothing

**Finding (2026-08-11 overnight, rate-based LL-B, pre-registered,
10 paired replicates x 3 arms):** ratification rates — SEED 1/10,
UNDIRECTED 1/10, **DIRECTED 4/10** (directed proposal rate 1.00; floors
met; verdict per fixed rules: SUPPORTED, directional). The undirected
arm's flatness is the finding's teeth: **global enrichment left the
ratification rate exactly at the threshold-stochastic base rate — the
directed term did ALL the work.** Enrichment ≠ concentration (F18
impl. 4) is now a controlled comparative result, not an observation.

**Implications:**
1. **The living loop closes, in rate form:** generation growth funds
   vocabulary — WHEN aimed where the language is trying to speak.
   T153's complexity ratchet works as a directed process; the
   curriculum-backlog principle (grow the world toward the pending
   demand) is validated at the vocabulary level, inside one organism.
2. **Demand-weighted allocation gets its first billed receipt** (the
   law's sixth appearance, first comparative confirmation): a funding
   economy that aims its generation at its own pending questions
   quadruples its learning rate; one that merely grows does not learn
   faster at all.
3. **EL-P1's design decision is made:** the living-language arm must
   use directed slack — an undirected living language would likely
   match frozen controls (the undirected arm predicts it), and a
   failed EL-P1 with undirected generation would have been a design
   artifact, not a theory test. F19 just saved EL-P1 from its own
   most likely false negative.
4. **T154's coupling demonstrated intra-membrane:** "upward flow
   terminates as demand on complexity pressure" was registered for
   organism→environment; F19 shows the same mechanism working
   language→world inside the environment itself. The demand-alignment
   experiment (booked) asks whether the two membranes' demands are one.

**Caveats billed with the verdict:** directional first bill exactly at
the 4/10 floor; one target word (the 7x-confirmed candidate — fixed a
priori, but generalization to other pending words untested); one
bootstrap policy; n=10.

### F20 [W] SOV-P1 first bill: in a world that drifts, there is no safe time to close

**Finding (2026-08-11 overnight, sov_p1_experiment.py v4, one stream /
two accountants, pre-registered): PARTIAL — one of two endpoints.**
- **Account accuracy (fresh tightness): SOV decisively better, both
  phases.** Pre-shift: SOV 0.207 vs FORCED 0.512; post-shift: 0.309 vs
  0.569. The eager-commitment web's frozen resolutions drifted from
  reality IMMEDIATELY — before the designed shift ever arrived —
  because tier-4 sources move continuously; the tier change only
  widened the gap.
- **Calibration: FORCED narrowly better (0.210 vs 0.243)** — with an
  identified mechanical entanglement: closed-K certainty refreshes at a
  fixed recency (0.05 alpha on confirms) while old open-slot certainty
  updates on every positive fit at the floored alpha with score inputs
  — the endpoint partially compares certainty-update RULES, not
  lifecycles. Export: disentangle before the next bill.
- Treatment floors met (9 forced closures standing; n_fresh in the
  thousands both arms after the endpoint-semantics fix: a K's fresh
  distance = distance of lived reality from the FROZEN account).

**Implications:**
1. **Theorem 2's "commitment pays while the world holds" clause is
   EMPTY in ERTI-class worlds** — the world never holds at slot grain
   (sources drift within-tier; F13 already said no closure survives).
   Commitment loses on account-accuracy continuously, not just at
   shifts: *in a world that drifts, there is no safe time to close.*
   The two findings are one: F13 showed the lifecycle refuses to stay
   closed; F20 shows what staying closed costs.
2. The instrument arc itself billed: three UNTESTED verdicts caught by
   floors (zero treatment, thin treatment, survivor-biased endpoint) —
   C20's checks 5 and 6 each fired once, and the endpoint-semantics fix
   (frozen-account distance) is now core, battery-covered.
3. Owed for the full bill: a calibration endpoint that doesn't entangle
   update rules; a shift class aimed AT committed dimensions
   (adversarial commitment-breaking, CV-P1 inversion style); and the
   deep test — consequence-level endpoints (the web driving decisions),
   which is Theorem 1 + Theorem 2 jointly, post-replay-phase.

**Follow-on implications (2026-08-11 morning, F19+F20 held together):**
4. **Closure belongs to the tower and to relations, not positions**
   (F20 x F15's inverted gradient): the forced web's commitments were
   POSITIONAL (centroids — maximally drift-exposed); relations survive
   source motion, invariants live one level up. PRE-REGISTERED for the
   replay build: the first SURVIVING closures will be relational and
   meta-level — F13's empty class fills from above and from between,
   never from places. Ks are earned laws, not frozen coordinates.
5. **The co-complexification treadmill, mechanically specified:** F19
   (drift toward pending demands) + F20 (track drift without
   commitment) are jointly load-bearing — directed drift shatters
   committed learners; open lifecycles in undirected worlds stagnate.
   EL-P1's living arm IS the treadmill; its frozen control lacks both
   halves.
6. **'Certainty' is a conflated word in sov.py** — open-slot
   score-EMA and closed-K confirm-refresh are two receipt processes
   wearing one name (polysemy by our own definition, found by our own
   court the same week EL-2 found the environment's). Split before the
   next calibration bill: support (funded recency) vs reliability
   (predictive track record).
7. **Richness without aim = generation-side prediction-carving**
   (F19's flat arm x Theorem 1): structure added without consequence-
   aiming doesn't move the learnable frontier — P79's stakes condition
   gains an in-house receipt.
8. **One center of gravity:** the replay phase now carries SOV-P3,
   P60, split-reduces-churn, P76/P77, consequence-level SOV-P1, AND
   the relational-closure prediction — and demand alignment folds into
   its instrumented runs at near-zero marginal cost.
9. External correspondence (named): **memory reconsolidation** (Nader
   et al.) — biological memory is rewritten on recall, every retrieval
   a reopening — F20's conclusion in tissue: ledgers that cannot move
   rot, so the brain's refuses permanent closure too.

### F21 [W] The replay phase's first bill: the rent economy prunes composition exactly as the algebra requires — and churn needs a world that stays

**Run:** `replay_overnight.py` (2026-08-11, card locked pre-launch in
its docstring + replay_phase_requirements.md; results
`results/replay_phase_overnight.json`). 24 generations x 2 tiered
worlds x 4 episodes x 600 steps; 4.47M fits, 1.67M receipts; scans at
every boundary; encoder rebuilt each generation on the run's own lived
log; conservation clean x24 (after fixing the auditor itself — below).

**P69 (registry; the stub's "P60"): SUPPORTED, decisively.** 24
composed slots; survival fraction 0.125 (LOW — 21 of 24 rent-evicted);
survivors' mean post-create positive fits 89,600 vs evicted mean 7,314
(12x — SELECTIVE). Diagnostic D-C1 lands exactly as Two-Sorted Core §2
predicted: endogenous composition is combinatorial, and rent-plus-
eviction is what prunes it — survival is selection by lived funding,
not TTL (rent funding live since 2026-08-10). The composition scan
proposed a mediator every single boundary; the economy kept 3.

**Scan census:** Compose 24/24 boundaries; Unify 16 (the geometry-
identity gate passed 16 times — the web actively coarsens while
composition enriches); Individuate 0 successes with the unassigned
pool ENDING AT ZERO (the inherited vocabulary covers these worlds —
web-level 404s are rare here, consistent with F13's organism); Exclude
0 (divergence never reached floor with edges already present);
Differentiate 0 — because churn never accumulated.

**The three UNTESTED verdicts are one diagnosis.** Split-reduces-churn,
relational closure, and demand alignment all wait on closure churn, and
the run produced exactly ONE closure attempt (gen 0, `seq_buf`, under
'while a predator sweeps' + 'when energy is low' + 'close to a wall')
against the seed-44 baseline's 195. The regime difference is now
isolated: deep-time's worlds PERSIST across generations — the organism
re-lives the same world while the encoder drifts, so slot distributions
stabilize enough to close, and THEN the world moves and the 404 window
reopens them. This run changed both worlds every generation (48
distinct worlds), so the lived distribution never stood still long
enough to close on. Churn is not a function of exposure volume (4.47M
fits!) but of world persistence: **closure needs a world that stays;
reopening needs a world that then moves.** F20 said there is no safe
time to close in a world that drifts; F21 adds the complement — in a
world that never repeats, there is nothing to close ON.

**Two instrument lessons, both billed:** (1) closure dynamics live in
the organism's OWN lived embedding space — two smoke configs with
detached boot-data encoders produced zero attempts at any radius;
the lived-log rebuild engaged the phenomenon in smoke gen 1 (C20
check 5 earned through three instrument iterations). (2) The
conservation auditor itself false-positived 8 times at mass ~5x10^5:
absolute tolerance 1e-6 on two sums accumulated in different float
orders. Fixed to relative tolerance (sov.py; battery 148/148). The
auditor of the books needs books-scale arithmetic.

**Implications (2026-08-11):**
1. **The algebra's inflation control is real** — P69 was the phase's
   first billable prediction and its confirmation is the license for
   the second wave: P76/P77 and the imagination workspace needed edge
   density, and the web now carries 408 edges (from ~0 pre-phase).
2. **A regime v2 card is owed for the churn-dependent endpoints:**
   persistent worlds re-lived across generations with pre-registered
   shift points (the deep-time signature at session scale). The three
   UNTESTED verdicts stay honest until that run.
3. **The world-persistence requirement is itself a T153 datum:** churn
   as world-regime clock presupposes regimes — worlds that hold and
   then break. A world resampled every generation has no regimes, so
   the non-stationarity reading of churn could never have been tested
   here even if splits had fired. The acceptance discriminator NEEDS
   the v2 regime to be fair to its rival.
4. **Unify's 16 admissions are unexamined structure** — the web merged
   16 slot pairs by geometry identity; whether those merges were
   synonymy (EL-2's co-extension lesson) or conflation is exactly the
   split-reduces-churn question pointed the other way. The v2 run
   should track merged-slot churn too.

### F22 [W] The first surviving closure in program history — and it is positional, not relational

**Run:** `replay_overnight_v2.py` (2026-08-11, persistent-world regime:
three regimes of 8 generations, shifts at 8 and 16; card locked at
launch; results `results/replay_phase_v2.json`). Conservation clean
x24 (relative-tolerance auditor's first full run).

**F13's empty class is FILLED.** Slot `formalization` (inherited,
positional) closed at generation 19 and STAYED CLOSED through run end —
5 generations, ~24,000 subsequent fits, 5 encoder rebases. The web now
holds one assertion — the first content an LC readout could ever state
rather than ask. The precondition F13 impl. 8 named is met.

**F20's relational-closure prediction: NOT SUPPORTED (earned).** The
pre-registered claim — the first surviving closures would be
operator-born (Compose/Differentiate/Individuate/Abstract), not
inherited-positional — is falsified at this bill: the first survivor is
an inherited family slot. One mechanical caveat carried honestly: the
Compose geometry (averaged parent thresholds) builds BROAD mediators —
the three survivors fire on essentially every step (fit counts ~ full
residual exposure), so their radius EMA converges to the mean distance
of a wide distribution and can hardly close by construction. The
prediction's fair test at the relational level may require Constrain
traffic narrowing composed slots first; at THIS grain, with THIS
compose, position closed first. The tower reading survives as an open
question, not as a supported claim.

**P69 replicates exactly** (24 composed, survival 0.125, survivor mean
fits 89,600 vs evicted 7,314 — the same selection signature under a
different world regime), with the sharper reading above: composition
survives only when the mediator fires constantly; rent starves every
occasional mediator.

**Closure is threshold-stochastic and SLOW — the shift schedule was
mistimed, not the mechanism.** Persistence did its job (smoke closed a
slot by gen 2 in one realization; the full run closed at gen 19 —
near-threshold events under nondeterministic encoder training, F18's
ratification lesson again), but both pre-registered shifts (8, 16) fell
BEFORE anything was closed, so nothing could reopen: total churn stayed
at 1 event and split-reduces-churn + demand alignment remain UNTESTED.
The run also ended before the survivor ever faced a shift — its
survival is rebase-tested (real: 5 epochs), not shift-tested.

**A quiet first elsewhere in the same run:** the court ratified its
first word ACROSS a regime shift — 'near a pain source' split by
discriminator 'when energy is low', proposed in regime 1's last
generation, ratified on regime 2's genuinely new worlds. The junction
law's strongest form (new worlds, not just new episodes) fired once
and passed.

**Implications (2026-08-11):**
1. **The LC's gate is open.** The web holds an assertion. P72 is no
   longer vacuous; task #44 (language center) is unblocked at the
   design level — its first utterance has a referent.
2. **A v3 card is owed with the timing fixed:** closures form at
   gen ~15-20 under this regime, so regimes must be LONGER (persist
   until closure, then shift — e.g., shift at 20) or the run longer,
   with at least one shift AFTER first closure so the 404 window,
   reopen churn, split-reduces-churn, and demand alignment all get
   their phenomenon. The survivor's shift-test is the same run.
3. **Compose needs Constrain traffic** before the relational-closure
   question can be fairly re-asked: broad mediators cannot close by
   construction; narrowing is the missing operator flow (and P59's
   liability bookkeeping already prices it).
4. **Two threshold-stochastic courts, one lesson (F18 -> closure):**
   single-run presence/absence of near-threshold events is noise;
   rates over replicates are the honest grain for closure too.

**Follow-on implications (2026-08-11, the generative question):**
5. **The triptych completes, and the quiet half is a confirmation:**
   F20 — in a world that drifts there is no safe time to close; F21 —
   in a world that never repeats there is nothing to close on; F22 —
   when the world holds still, closure happens, AND the earned K
   survives five recoordinatizations. That last clause is the
   ledgerless economy's "ripple damage" pathology answered in-house:
   five encoder epochs, one account — the support-ring etymology made
   the web re-basable exactly as designed. Retraining did not eat the
   commitment.
6. **A de-conflation inside the falsification** — the card measured
   ORIGIN; the prediction was about CONTENT. The slot that closed is
   `formalization` (channels 58-61: boundary, exception,
   rule-extraction, rule-revision) — the inherited vocabulary's most
   relation-like word. F20's letter failed (operator-born vs
   inherited); its spirit — closure favors invariant, lawlike content
   over coordinates — survives with the operationalization corrected.
   v3 pre-registers the content-level form: closures concentrate in
   families detecting invariant structure (T95 trunk membership as the
   predictor — the program's oldest result predicting its newest
   census). Noted without over-reading: the first thing the organism
   settled, in the first regime that held still, is its account of the
   world's rules.
7. **The economy funds contact, not information.** P69's survivors
   are always-on mediators: rent credit per fit makes a slot that
   fires on everything rent-immortal and unfalsifiable — while
   Constrain, the narrowing flow, has NO traffic in the driver (the
   monotone fragment's unused operator). Before the relational
   question is re-asked: either fit credit weighted by discrimination
   (selectivity-priced rent) or Constrain scans narrowing composed
   slots. Design proposal, awaiting the user — the mechanism it
   touches is currently working.
8. **Genesis endpoints demand rate designs — a methodological law.**
   F18 (ratification) and F22 (closure) are the same lesson at two
   layers: every receipts-gated genesis event is rare BY CONSTRUCTION
   (the gate exists to make it rare), so single-run presence/absence
   is systematically underpowered for exactly the events the program
   most wants to see. Every future card with a genesis endpoint
   (closure, ratification, individuation, phrase birth) books rates
   over replicates or books UNTESTED in advance.
9. **The first glimmer of cross-membrane alignment, stated and not
   billed:** 'when energy is low' surfaced independently in both
   membranes' first genesis events — as the discriminator of the
   court's first cross-shift ratified word AND as a context of the
   organism's sole churn event. n=1 on each side, floors unmet,
   observation only — but it is exactly the shape an aligned demand
   ledger would show at birth.
10. **Epistemic maturity is now a measurable scalar:** the assertive
    fraction — assertable Ks over total slots (today: 1/41 ~ 0.024).
    The LC's first utterance is fully determined by the ledger: one
    assertion, and well-formed questions for everything else. Track
    the fraction per run; it is the readout's speakable ratio and the
    web's coming-of-age curve.

**The environment-evolution implication (2026-08-11, the user's
question: what does F22 imply about how the environment should
evolve?):**
11. **Punctuated equilibrium is the teaching rhythm — derived, not
    chosen.** The triptych fixes the environment-organism's temporal
    grammar: continuous drift teaches only open accounts (F20);
    perpetual novelty teaches nothing closable (F21); so the world
    that maximizes what its students can KNOW must hold still until
    accounts close, then move. T153's complexity pressure gains a
    pacing constraint: the environment's evolution rate is bounded by
    its inhabitants' closure rate. Grow faster than students close and
    nothing settles; freeze and surprise flux flatlines (stagnation,
    T153's named failure mode). The immortal teacher holds each lesson
    until it is learned, then changes it.
12. **The closure ledger is the curriculum clock — WHEN to shift is
    now readable telemetry.** Shift when churn dies and the assertive
    fraction rises (the world has been accounted); hold while churn
    is live (the world is still being learned). This composes with
    F19 into a complete editorial policy for the living language:
    mutate WHERE the language strains (directed slack at pending
    demands), WHEN the accounts settle (closure-gated timing). Two
    gates, one policy — spatial direction from the court, temporal
    direction from the web.
13. **Change what the student knows; hold what the student is still
    learning.** A shift aimed at CLOSED structure is guaranteed-
    learnable surprise: the student had it right, the 404 says exactly
    where to look, and the reopened slot relearns with full vocabulary
    — F22's own emblem: `formalization` closed on the world's rules,
    so the teaching move is to change the rules. A shift aimed at
    unaccounted structure may not even register as surprise — it is
    noise to a web with no slot for it. The closure census therefore
    marks WHERE produced surprise will be learnable, which is the
    'learnable' in T153's objective made mechanical. This inverts
    naive curriculum design (novelty everywhere): stability is as
    load-bearing as change, and it belongs over the open slots. And it
    is NOT the mirror trap: the environment reads the inhabitants'
    accounting state to time and place its moves, never their
    structure to copy — it is funded by the description-error
    potential of accounts about to be made wrong, the anti-echo.
14. **Both courts share one tempo — co-complexification is temporally
    coupled.** The lexical court's own junction law needed the same
    rhythm (same-world episodes to propose, genuinely new worlds to
    ratify — the first cross-shift ratification happened BECAUSE the
    regime held and then broke), and the web's closure lifecycle needs
    it too. The environment's shift schedule is the shared clock of
    both membranes' ledgers; EL-P1's world lineages must persist
    across generations (mutation rate << learning rate, T153's
    constitutional timescale separation, with F22 supplying the
    constant: hold >= closure time, ~15-20 generations at current
    scale) and aim mutations at settled content.

### F23 [W] The orphaned commitment: under a full world change, a closed account goes silent, not wrong — and the 404 fires only on contact

**Run:** `replay_overnight_v3.py` (2026-08-11, closure-paced curriculum,
36 gens; card locked at launch; results `results/replay_phase_v3.json`).
Conservation clean x36. The curriculum clock WORKED as specified: four
closure-paced shifts (gens 5/11/17/23), zero fallbacks — the first run
whose world moved because its student had settled.

**Headline verdicts per the locked card:** shift-test 2x2 NOT SUPPORTED
(earned); P69 SUPPORTED a third time; split-reduces-churn and demand
alignment UNTESTED; relational closure UNTESTED at run end (the K
reopened at gen 26 and never re-closed — v2's filled class refers to
v2's run end; each run bills its own).

**The discovery inside the NOT SUPPORTED.** `formalization` closed at
gen 2 and "survived" shifts 1-3 with ZERO post-shift fits: the reseeded
worlds never activated its boundary, so `_fit_closed` never ran, the
404 window never got jurisdiction, and the K sat closed for 20+ idle
generations. It was not confirmed and not refuted — it was ORPHANED:
the world's motion made the account unreachable rather than wrong.
When a post-shift world finally touched it (shift 4: 19 fits), it
reopened at latency 2 — the variant-cell prediction exactly, in the
single trial where the test's implicit precondition (contact) held.
The card required contact for the invariant cell (>= 12 fits) and
forgot it for the variant cell; the three "failures" are
unassessable-by-design. NOT SUPPORTED stands per the card's own rules;
the corrected form — latency measured from FIRST POST-SHIFT CONTACT,
contact-gated assessment in BOTH cells — is pre-registered here for
any successor run.

**Implications (2026-08-11):**
1. **Stranded commitments have two species, and F20 only named one.**
   WRONG commitments (F20: the world moved and the account misleads)
   and ORPHANED commitments (F23: the world moved and the account is
   unreachable — no fits, no falsification, no rent income, invisible
   to every truth-maintenance mechanism that runs on contact). The
   ledgerless economy's pathology list gains an entry: an account no
   query can reach is epistemically dead while formally alive, and
   NOTHING in the current algebra distinguishes it from a settled
   truth. Candidate mechanism (design proposal, user's call): a
   staleness clock on closed slots — a K unexercised for N generations
   loses assertion rights (demoted to 'dormant': citable history, not
   citable truth) without reopening; contact restores it. Assertion
   rights should require BOTH funding and recency of contact.
2. **The curriculum clock read the wrong register — 'settled' must
   mean settled-AND-IN-CONTACT.** The clock kept shifting on a
   standing K that was actually orphaned: assertive fraction counted
   an account the world could no longer reach. The corrected clock
   reads LIVE closures (closed AND recently fitting); same fix as
   impl. 1, same register.
3. **The 404 mechanism itself is now positively evidenced:** one
   contact-trial, one correct fast reopen (latency 2). Small n, right
   sign, and the mechanism's first clean observation under a real
   world shift.
4. **Full reseed is the wrong shift instrument for the shift-test.**
   Changing EVERYTHING mostly orphans; the informative shift PERTURBS
   the structure a K is about while keeping the K reachable — which is
   EL-2.5's mutation operator, not a new seed. The shift-test's fair
   venue is the living-language world lineage (targeted mutations,
   F22 impl. 13's 'change the rules, keep the world'), fusing the
   replay phase and the EL arc into one experiment for the first time
   by NECESSITY, not convenience.
5. **The two membranes want different tempos — a real tension, named.**
   Long persistence enabled closure but STARVED the court: zero
   proposals in 36 generations (homogeneous windows -> no description
   failures -> silence; v2's single proposal already hinted). The
   organism's closure clock says hold; the language's proposal engine
   says vary. Co-complexification (F22 impl. 14) is not one shared
   tempo but a NEGOTIATION between two — the environment must schedule
   both stability (for closure) and variety (for description failure),
   which is exactly what a mutation-based lineage (small changes,
   persistent identity) provides and full reseed cannot. Convergent
   with impl. 4: both point at EL-2.5 worlds as the next venue.
6. **P69 is now a law-grade regularity:** three regimes, three
   confirmations, same signature (survival ~0.125, selectivity ~12x).
   Composition's economy needs no further single-run bills; its next
   appearance should be as an assumed invariant in someone else's
   card.

### F24 [W] The treadmill's deadlock mode — and the law we billed yesterday convicting the card we wrote today

**Run:** `replay_overnight_v4.py` (2026-08-11, the fusion: closure
lifecycle inside mutating EL-2.5 corpus lineages; 36 gens, 6.9M fits;
results `results/replay_phase_v4.json`). Conservation clean x36. P69's
signature a fourth time (36 composed — law-grade, unbilled). All five
card endpoints UNTESTED: ZERO closure attempts in 36 generations.

**The deadlock, mechanically produced.** v4 coupled the two tempos
completely: mutations fire only on shift; shifts fire only on live
closure (fallback 24). No closure ever formed in these lineages, so the
world froze for 24 generations, so the court starved (1 proposal), so
the whole treadmill stalled at step one. F23 named the tempo TENSION;
F24 exhibits the tempo DEADLOCK: **if the student never settles, the
world never moves, and both starve.** This is T153's stagnation failure
mode produced not by a flat world but by OVER-COUPLING — the caregiver
who waits forever for a lesson that this student, in this world, was
never going to finish.

**The method failure, owned per C20's booking rule.** F22 impl. 8 —
genesis endpoints demand rate designs — was billed BEFORE this card was
written, and the card still gated five endpoints on a single-run
genesis event. Worse, check 5's "closure demonstrated in v2/v3" was an
EXISTENCE receipt from DIFFERENT worlds: closure propensity turns out
to be world-dependent (v3's lineage closed at gen 2; v2's at gen 19;
v4's corpus lineages never in 36), so a phenomenon-strength receipt
does not transfer across world sources. A deficiency the card's own
checks should have caught, found after 41 minutes of compute: method
failure, recorded.

**Implications (2026-08-11):**
1. **The treadmill needs a liveness floor — the environment must have
   its own metabolism.** A base mutation rate (a metronome: one
   mutation per lineage every M generations, regardless of the
   student) with closure-paced shifts as ADDITIONAL punctuation. The
   student's clock modulates the world's tempo; it must never own it
   outright. Real environments change on their own schedule; the
   caregiver adjusts pace but never stops the world.
2. **C20 check 5 must be re-earned per world source.** Existence
   receipts for threshold-stochastic phenomena are distribution-bound.
   When the world source changes (direct env -> corpus lineage), the
   smoke must re-demonstrate the phenomenon THERE, or the card books
   its endpoints as conditional.
3. **Closability is a world property, and it is measurable.** Which
   worlds afford settling — at this learner's capacity, under this
   encoder — varied from gen-2 to never across three world sources.
   That is the environment-side dual of the assertive fraction, and a
   curriculum needs closable lessons before pacing means anything.
   Cheap probe: 6-8 gens, count closure attempts; screen lineages
   before committing a full run. (Flagged, not concluded: the EL-0
   round trip was bit-identical on its acceptance worlds; whether
   corpus interpretation subtly shifts the lived distribution for
   OTHER worlds is now an open instrument question.)
4. **v5 derives itself:** mutation metronome (M=6) + closure-paced
   extra shifts; closability-screened lineages; rate design for every
   genesis endpoint (shorter replicates, presence measured as a rate).
   The fusion architecture stands — worlds as lineages, live-closure
   clock, contact-gated shift-test all survived contact with reality;
   only the coupling and the booking were wrong.

**Follow-on implications (2026-08-11, the generative question):**
5. **Autonomy is load-bearing — the 'organism' in environment-organism
   is a requirement, not a metaphor.** v4 accidentally demoted the
   environment from organism to instrument (a machine triggered by the
   student's clock) and the ecology died. Mutual Umwelt requires
   mutual autonomy: a coupled learning pair where one side's motion is
   fully slaved to the other's progress deadlocks on the slow case —
   said plainly it is almost a tautology; v4's value is that the
   program LIVED it at a cost of 41 minutes rather than a career. For
   the ecology to run, both parties need endogenous dynamics; the
   student's telemetry may modulate the world's tempo but must never
   own it.
6. **The mirror trap has a twin, and the pair bounds the design
   space.** T153 named the environment's content failure (the MIRROR
   TRAP: refine wherever the student refines — an echo). F24 names its
   tempo failure (the WAITING TRAP: move only when the student
   settles — a freeze). Both are failures of environmental autonomy —
   one in what it says, one in when it acts. Teaching = autonomous
   generation, disciplined but not enslaved by student state, on both
   axes. With F19's flat arm this completes the teacher's job
   description: the environment needs its own motion (F24), aim
   (F19), and pacing discipline (F22) — any one alone fails in a
   now-named way.
7. **Closability completes the curriculum triad — and marks the
   growth frontier.** F19: WHERE to enrich. F22: WHEN to change. F24:
   WHAT can be settled at all — closability, per world x learner
   capacity, the quantity T157's capacity-bounded carving theorem
   governs. Prediction worth carrying: the productive curriculum sits
   at the closability MARGIN — worlds barely closable at current
   capacity (the zone of proximal development, formalized as
   closability-at-margin). A curriculum of trivially closable worlds
   ratchets nothing; unclosable worlds (v4's, apparently) ratchet
   nothing; the frontier is the band between.
8. **The bookkeeping-lag principle bit the method itself (T154,
   reflexive).** F22 impl. 8 existed in the ledger and was not cited
   by the very next card — a lesson that is not wired into the
   pre-flight CHECKLIST is functionally absent at booking time (the
   junction law's third clause: receipts must be ADDRESSABLE).
   Concrete amendment owed to constraints.md, awaiting the user: C20
   gains (a) genesis endpoints must be rate-designed or booked
   conditional; (b) phenomenon-strength receipts are
   distribution-bound — re-earn check 5 when the world source
   changes. Until the checklist holds them, the lessons don't exist
   where booking happens.
9. **A new instrument class, noticed:** the closability probe measures
   a property OF A WORLD using organism telemetry — T157's
   animate-to-understand move pointed not outward at weather but
   inward at the curriculum's own worlds. The stakeholder instrument
   auditing its own school.

### F25 [W] The metronome cures the deadlock; accounts are mutation-robust; and the two membranes are separated by a dose gap

**Run:** `replay_overnight_v5.py` x3 + `replay_v5_analyze.py` (2026-08-11;
rate design per C20 check 7; lineage pair screened CLOSABLE by
`closability_probe.py` — census: 4/4 candidate pairs closable, the
round-trip instrument exonerated, v4's zero fully attributed to
realization stochasticity x deadlock). Results
`results/replay_phase_v5_pooled.json`.

**The deadlock is cured: closure presence 3/3.** Every replicate closed
a K under the metronome regime (M=6, one mutation/lineage, plus
closure-paced punctuation), with ZERO orphaned standing-Ks and zero
dormancy events across 54 generations — the mutation shifts kept every
account in contact, exactly as the fusion design intended. The
environment's own pulse plus the student's modulation is a LIVE
ecology; v4's waiting trap does not recur.

**The same slot closes every time.** `formalization` (boundary,
exception, rule-extraction, rule-revision) is the first — and only —
closure in all three replicates, as it was in v2, v3, and probe
realizations: five-plus independent realizations across three world
sources. At this capacity, in these worlds, the organism's first
settled knowledge is ALWAYS its account of the world's rules. F22's
"noted without over-reading" is now a regularity with a receipt trail.

**Shift-test: NOT SUPPORTED — earned this time, and the failure has
structure.** Seven contact-gated variant-cell trials (fits_post >= 12,
nothing orphaned — the F23 instrument critique fully addressed), and
the K failed to reopen within 2 gens of contact in ALL seven. Under
metronome-dose mutations (add a barrier, add a source), the closed
account simply KEEPS BEING RIGHT — while v3 showed the same slot
reopening at latency 2 under full reseed. So account fragility has a
DOSE-RESPONSE: small world edits do not falsify a rule-structure K;
only large structural change does. And the 2x2's content-invariance
axis (family activation variance ACROSS WORLDS) does not predict
robustness AT MUTATION SCALE — wrong scale of variation, a classifier
lesson worth keeping.

**The court is still silent — and the excuse is gone.** Zero proposals
across 54 generations WITH a moving world. v4 blamed the frozen world;
v5 moved the world 5+ times per replicate and the court still starved.
The mutation dose that keeps accounts reachable is too small to
generate description failures; the world-batch variety that makes the
court speak (F19's fresh corpora batches) is reseed-dose. **The two
membranes are separated by a dose gap at current scale**: no single
world-change size currently serves both ledgers. Demand alignment
stays honestly UNTESTED (pooled churn 6 < 10, proposals 0 < 5).

**Implications (2026-08-11):**
1. **The tempo negotiation has become a dose negotiation** — F23
   named two tempos; F25 shows the deeper variable is world-change
   MAGNITUDE, and the two ledgers' useful ranges do not currently
   overlap. The obvious next card: a dose-response sweep (mutations
   per tick: 1, 4, 8, 16 ... reseed) measuring reopen rate AND
   proposal rate per dose — either the curves cross (an operating
   band exists: the co-complexification treadmill has a working gear)
   or they don't (the membranes need different world-change species,
   e.g. court trained across lineage HISTORY rather than within
   generations).
2. **Closure robustness is evidence about WHAT was learned.** A K
   that survives barrier-additions but breaks under reseed is an
   account of deep structure, not surface configuration — the
   dose-response curve of a K is a free measurement of its content's
   depth. (T157's D-metric, showing up as fragility spectroscopy.)
3. **`formalization`-first is now a prediction, not an observation:**
   any closable world at this capacity closes the rule-structure
   family first. Falsifiable in any future run; carried forward.
4. **The instrument stack is done iterating:** probes (5 min) catch
   unclosable worlds; the metronome guarantees liveness; dormancy
   guards orphaning; contact-gating makes shift-tests fair; rate
   design makes genesis billable. Five findings' worth of method is
   now standing equipment.

### F26 [W] The dose-response sweep: the court has a threshold, the web has a corridor — and the treadmill's gear is a mutation SPECIES, not a dose

**Run:** `dose_sweep.py` x10 (5 doses x 2 replicates, pair B, ~200 min
compute) + `dose_sweep_analyze.py` (2026-08-11; card locked at launch;
results `results/dose_sweep_pooled.json`). Rate-designed per C20-7.

**The three curves:**
- COURT: a clean monotone threshold — silent at doses 1 and 4, speaks
  at 8, 16, and reseed (proposal rate 0.056/gen at each). The court's
  activation dose is ~8 mutations of the current species.
- WEB (reopen): ZERO reopens at EVERY mutation dose, including 16 —
  standing Ks stayed in contact (orphan rate 0.0 across all mutation
  doses) and simply kept being right. At reseed: orphan rate 1.0 —
  every standing K lost contact within the 3-gen window. Reopen was
  never observed anywhere in the sweep.
- ORPHANING is a step function: 0.0 at all mutation doses, 1.0 at
  reseed. (Dormancy fired in the field exactly as built: the reseed
  arm's orphaned K demoted, reawakened on brief contact, demoted
  again — the F23 mechanism breathing.)

**Verdict per the locked band rule: PARTIAL-RESPONSE / NO overlapping
band** — web responds nowhere, court responds at >= 8. But the shape of
the failure is the discovery:

**The falsification corridor is narrow BY CONSTRUCTION.** Closure's
admission gate (radius <= 0.05 over the lived distribution) only admits
accounts robust to the lineage's own within-world variation — so
sub-reseed change CANNOT churn a K (the account was admitted precisely
because such change doesn't move it), while reseed-scale change removes
jurisdiction (F23's orphaning). The world changes that could falsify an
account are mostly the ones that make it unreachable. v3's single
observed reopen was the lucky middle: a reseed that happened to keep
the K's activation reachable. Selection effect, stated as law:
**closure selects for mutation-robust content, therefore churn requires
change at the K's own depth, delivered reachably** — and the current
mutation vocabulary cannot produce that.

**The gear is a mutation SPECIES, not a dose.** EL-2.5's mutation
operators (add source, add barrier, add trigger, perturb position) edit
the world's FURNITURE. `formalization`'s content is the world's RULES.
No quantity of furniture edits touches a rule account, and F22 impl. 13
already named the needed move ("change the rules, keep the world"): the
mutation vocabulary needs a LAWS stratum — rule-level operators
(change a trigger's period, alter gate conditions, modify hidden-state
dynamics) that perturb the decree stratum's dynamics while preserving
the world's addresses. T153's decree/description split, arriving in
the mutation operator set.
